km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    stream = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n\n    print(\"Loading station metadata...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n\n    return stream, stations_df\n\ndef prepare_station_data(stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Prepare station metadata for processing.\"\"\"\n    # Create a mapping of (network, station, location, channel) to coordinates\n    stations_df = stations_df.fillna('')\n    station_coords = {}\n\n    for idx, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = (row['network'], row['station'], row['location'], row['channel'])\n        station_coords[key] = {\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'elevation': row['elevation_m'],\n            'response': row['response']\n        }\n\n    return station_coords\n\ndef pick_phases(stream):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using SeisBench PhaseNet model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\n    model = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for trace in stream:\n        try:\n            net = trace.stats.network\n            sta = trace.stats.station\n            loc = trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else ''\n            chan = trace.stats.channel\n\n            print(f\"  Picking phases for {net}.{sta}.{loc}.{chan}\")\n\n            # Get prediction from model\n            pred = model.predict(trace)\n\n            # Extract pick information\n            for phase_type in ['P', 'S']:\n                if phase_type in pred:\n                    phase_data = pred[phase_type]\n                    # Find the peak in the probability\n                    peak_idx = np.argmax(phase_data)\n                    if phase_data[peak_idx] > 0.1:  # confidence threshold\n                        pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                        picks.append({\n                            'network': net,\n                            'station': sta,\n                            'location': loc,\n                            'channel': chan,\n                            'phase': phase_type,\n                            'time': pick_time,\n                            'confidence': float(phase_data[peak_idx])\n                        })\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Error processing trace: {e}\")\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases_alternative(stream):\n    \"\"\"Alternative phase picking using EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    stations = {}\n    for trace in stream:\n        sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station, trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n        if sta_key not in stations:\n            stations[sta_key] = []\n        stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n    # Process each station\n    for sta_key, traces in stations.items():\n        try:\n            net, sta, loc = sta_key\n            print(f\"  Picking phases for {net}.{sta}.{loc}\")\n\n            # Combine all 3 components into a stream\n            station_stream = stream.select(network=net, station=sta)\n            if loc:\n                station_stream = station_stream.select(location=loc)\n\n            if len(station_stream) > 0:\n                # Get prediction from model\n                pred = model.predict(station_stream)\n\n                # Extract pick information\n                if pred is not None and 'P' in pred:\n                    p_phase = pred['P']\n                    s_phase = pred['S']\n\n                    for trace in station_stream:\n                        chan = trace.stats.channel\n                        if chan.endswith('Z'):  # Use vertical component\n                            for phase_type, phase_data in [('P', p_phase), ('S', s_phase)]:\n                                if phase_data is not None:\n                                    peak_idx = np.argmax(phase_data)\n                                    if phase_data[peak_idx] > 0.1:\n                                        pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                                        picks.append({\n                                            'network': net,\n                                            'station': sta,\n                                            'location': loc,\n                                            'channel': chan,\n                                            'phase': phase_type,\n                                            'time': pick_time,\n                                            'confidence': float(phase_data[peak_idx])\n                                        })\n                            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Error processing station: {e}\")\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases_simple(stream):\n    \"\"\"Simple phase picking based on energy detection.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Using simple energy-based phase picking...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    stations = {}\n    for trace in stream:\n        sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station, trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n        if sta_key not in stations:\n            stations[sta_key] = []\n        stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n    # For each station, find picks from vertical component\n    for sta_key, station_traces in stations.items():\n        net, sta, loc = sta_key\n\n        # Find vertical component trace\n        z_trace = None\n        for trace in station_traces:\n            if trace.stats.channel.endswith('Z'):\n                z_trace = trace\n                break\n\n        if z_trace is None:\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate short-term average / long-term average (STA/LTA) ratio\n        data = z_trace.data.astype(float)\n\n        # Simple high-pass filter (remove DC and very low frequencies)\n        from scipy import signal\n        b, a = signal.butter(4, 0.05, btype='high')\n        filtered = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n\n        # Calculate energy\n        energy = np.abs(filtered)\n\n        # STA/LTA algorithm\n        lta_len = int(2 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 2 seconds\n        sta_len = int(0.5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n\n        lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n        sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n\n        ratio = np.divide(sta, lta, where=lta > 0, out=np.zeros_like(sta))\n\n        # Find peaks in the ratio (potential phase arrivals)\n        threshold = 2.5\n        peaks = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n        if len(peaks) > 0:\n            # Group nearby peaks\n            grouped_peaks = []\n            current_group = [peaks[0]]\n\n            for i in range(1, len(peaks)):\n                if peaks[i] - peaks[i-1] < z_trace.stats.sampling_rate:  # within 1 second\n                    current_group.append(peaks[i])\n                else:\n                    grouped_peaks.append(int(np.mean(current_group)))\n                    current_group = [peaks[i]]\n            grouped_peaks.append(int(np.mean(current_group)))\n\n            # Create a mapping of (network, station, location, channel) to coordinates\n    stations_df = stations_df.fillna('')\n    station_coords = {}\n\n    for idx, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        key = (row['network'], row['station'], row['location'], row['channel'])\n        station_coords[key] = {\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'elevation': row['elevation_m'],\n            'response': row['response']\n        }\n\n    return station_coords\n\ndef pick_phases(stream):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using SeisBench PhaseNet model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\n    model = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Process each trace\n    for trace in stream:\n        try:\n            net = trace.stats.network\n            sta = trace.stats.station\n            loc = trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else ''\n            chan = trace.stats.channel\n\n            print(f\"  Picking phases for {net}.{sta}.{loc}.{chan}\")\n\n            # Get prediction from model\n            pred = model.predict(trace)\n\n            # Extract pick information\n            for phase_type in ['P', 'S']:\n                if phase_type in pred:\n                    phase_data = pred[phase_type]\n                    # Find the peak in the probability\n                    peak_idx = np.argmax(phase_data)\n                    if phase_data[peak_idx] > 0.1:  # confidence threshold\n                        pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                        picks.append({\n                            'network': net,\n                            'station': sta,\n                            'location': loc,\n                            'channel': chan,\n                            'phase': phase_type,\n                            'time': pick_time,\n                            'confidence': float(phase_data[peak_idx])\n                        })\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Error processing trace: {e}\")\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases_alternative(stream):\n    \"\"\"Alternative phase picking using EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    stations = {}\n    for trace in stream:\n        sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station, trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n        if sta_key not in stations:\n            stations[sta_key] = []\n        stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n    # Process each station\n    for sta_key, traces in stations.items():\n        try:\n            net, sta, loc = sta_key\n            print(f\"  Picking phases for {net}.{sta}.{loc}\")\n\n            # Combine all 3 components into a stream\n            station_stream = stream.select(network=net, station=sta)\n            if loc:\n                station_stream = station_stream.select(location=loc)\n\n            if len(station_stream) > 0:\n                # Get prediction from model\n                pred = model.predict(station_stream)\n\n                # Extract pick information\n                if pred is not None and 'P' in pred:\n                    p_phase = pred['P']\n                    s_phase = pred['S']\n\n                    for trace in station_stream:\n                        chan = trace.stats.channel\n                        if chan.endswith('Z'):  # Use vertical component\n                            for phase_type, phase_data in [('P', p_phase), ('S', s_phase)]:\n                                if phase_data is not None:\n                                    peak_idx = np.argmax(phase_data)\n                                    if phase_data[peak_idx] > 0.1:\n                                        pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                                        picks.append({\n                                            'network': net,\n                                            'station': sta,\n                                            'location': loc,\n                                            'channel': chan,\n                                            'phase': phase_type,\n                                            'time': pick_time,\n                                            'confidence': float(phase_data[peak_idx])\n                                        })\n                            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Error processing station: {e}\")\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases_simple(stream):\n    \"\"\"Simple phase picking based on energy detection.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Using simple energy-based phase picking...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    stations = {}\n    for trace in stream:\n        sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station, trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n        if sta_key not in stations:\n            stations[sta_key] = []\n        stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n    # For each station, find picks from vertical component\n    for sta_key, station_traces in stations.items():\n        net, sta, loc = sta_key\n\n        # Find vertical component trace\n        z_trace = None\n        for trace in station_traces:\n            if trace.stats.channel.endswith('Z'):\n                z_trace = trace\n                break\n\n        if z_trace is None:\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate short-term average / long-term average (STA/LTA) ratio\n        data = z_trace.data.astype(float)\n\n        # Simple high-pass filter (remove DC and very low frequencies)\n        from scipy import signal\n        b, a = signal.butter(4, 0.05, btype='high')\n        filtered = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n\n        # Calculate energy\n        energy = np.abs(filtered)\n\n        # STA/LTA algorithm\n        lta_len = int(2 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 2 seconds\n        sta_len = int(0.5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n\n        lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n        sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n\n        ratio = np.divide(sta, lta, where=lta > 0, out=np.zeros_like(sta))\n\n        # Find peaks in the ratio (potential phase arrivals)\n        threshold = 2.5\n        peaks = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n        if len(peaks) > 0:\n            # Group nearby peaks\n            grouped_peaks = []\n            current_group = [peaks[0]]\n\n            for i in range(1, len(peaks)):\n                if peaks[i] - peaks[i-1] < z_trace.stats.sampling_rate:  # within 1 second\n                    current_group.append(peaks[i])\n                else:\n                    grouped_peaks.append(int(np.mean(current_group)))\n                    current_group = [peaks[i]]\n            grouped_peaks.append(int(np.mean(current_group)))\n\n            # Extract picks (P and S if we have multiple peaks)\n            for phase_idx, peak in enumerate(grouped_peaks[:2]):  # Use first 2 peaks as P and S\n                phase_type = 'P' if phase_idx == 0 else 'S'\n                pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + peak * z_trace.stats.delta\n                picks.append({\n                    'network': net,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'location': loc,\n                    'channel': z_trace.stats.channel,\n                    'phase': phase_type,\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'confidence': float(ratio[peak])\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_picks(picks, station_coords):\n    \"\"\"Associate picks into earthquake events using simple clustering.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nAssociating {len(picks)} picks into events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return []\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Convert time to seconds for clustering\n    picks_df['time_sec'] = picks_df['time'].apply(lambda t: (t - picks_df['time'].min()) * 1000)\n\n    # Convert time values to seconds from first pick\n    pick_times = np.array([float((t - picks_df['time'].min())) for t in picks_df['time']])\n\n    # Simple hierarchical clustering based on time\n    # Events should have picks within a few seconds of each other\n    events = []\n\n    if len(pick_times) > 0:\n        # Cluster picks by time\n        time_threshold = 10.0  # seconds - picks within 10 seconds are likely same event\n\n        # Sort by time\n        sorted_indices = np.argsort(pick_times)\n\n        current_event = [sorted_indices[0]]\n\n        for i in range(1, len(sorted_indices)):\n            idx = sorted_indices[i]\n            last_idx = current_event[-1]\n\n            if abs(pick_times[idx] - pick_times[last_idx]) < time_threshold:\n                current_event.append(idx)\n            else:\n                # Start new event\n                if len(current_event) >= 3:  # Need at least 3 picks for an event\n                    events.append(current_event)\n                current_event = [idx]\n\n        if len(current_event) >= 3:\n            events.append(current_event)\n\n    # Convert event indices to event information\n    event_list = []\n    for event_picks_indices in events:\n        event_picks = picks_df.iloc[event_picks_indices]\n\n        # Event time is the median of P-wave arrivals\n        p_picks = event_picks[event_picks['phase'] == 'P']\n        if len(p_picks) > 0:\n            event_time = p_picks['time'].median()\n        else:\n            event_time = event_picks['time'].min()\n\n        event_list.append({\n            'time': event_time,\n            'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n            'num_stations': len(event_picks['station'].unique())\n        })\n\n    return event_list\n\ndef write_results(events, output_file):\n    \"\"\"Write event results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to {output_file}...\")\n\n    if not events:\n        # Create empty results file\n        pd.DataFrame(columns=['time']).to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n        return\n\n    # Convert to DataFrame\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n\n    # Format time as ISO format without timezone\n    results_df['time'] = results_df['time'].apply(\n        lambda t: t.datetime.isoformat() if hasattr(t, 'datetime') else str(t)\n    )\n\n    # Select columns\n    output_df = results_df[['time']].copy()\n    if 'num_picks' in results_df.columns:\n        output_df['num_picks'] = results_df['num_picks']\n    if 'num_stations' in results_df.columns:\n        output_df['num_stations'] = results_df['num_stations']\n\n    output_df.to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n    print(f\"Results written to {output_file}\")\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stream)} traces\")\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n    station_coords = prepare_station_data(stations_df)\n\n    # Pick phases\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    try:\n        # Try using SeisBench models\n        picks = pick_phases_simple(stream)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error during phase picking: {e}\")\n        picks = []\n\n    print(f\"Picked {len(picks)} phases\")\n\n    if picks:\n        picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n        print(\"\\nPhase picks summary:\")\n        print(picks_df.groupby('phase').size())\n\n    # Associate picks into events\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"EVENT ASSOCIATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    events = associate_picks(picks, station_coords)\n    print(f\"Associated into {len(events)} events\")\n\n    if events:\n        print(\"\\nEvent summary:\")\n        events_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n        print(events_df)\n\n    # Write results\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"WRITING RESULTS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    write_results(events, '/root/results.csv')\n\n    print(\"\\nDone!\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":6,"oldLines":8,"newStart":6,"newLines":6,"lines":[" import numpy as np"," import pandas as pd"," from obspy import read","-from obspy.geodetics.base import calc_vincenty_distance","-import seisbench.models as sbm"," import warnings"," from datetime import datetime, timedelta"," from scipy.spatial.distance import pdist, squareform"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Get prediction from model\n            pred = model.predict(trace)\n\n            # Extract pick information\n            for phase_type in ['P', 'S']:\n                if phase_type in pred:\n                    phase_data = pred[phase_type]\n                    # Find the peak in the probability\n                    peak_idx = np.argmax(phase_data)\n                    if phase_data[peak_idx] > 0.1:  # confidence threshold\n                        pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                        picks.append({\n                            'network': net,\n                            'station': sta,\n                            'location': loc,\n                            'channel': chan,\n                            'phase': phase_type,\n                            'time': pick_time,\n                            'confidence': float(phase_data[peak_idx])\n                        })\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Error processing trace: {e}\")\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases_alternative(stream):\n    \"\"\"Alternative phase picking using EQTransformer model.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading SeisBench EQTransformer model...\")\n    model = sbm.EQTransformer.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    stations = {}\n    for trace in stream:\n        sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station, trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n        if sta_key not in stations:\n            stations[sta_key] = []\n        stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n    # Process each station\n    for sta_key, traces in stations.items():\n        try:\n            net, sta, loc = sta_key\n            print(f\"  Picking phases for {net}.{sta}.{loc}\")\n\n            # Combine all 3 components into a stream\n            station_stream = stream.select(network=net, station=sta)\n            if loc:\n                station_stream = station_stream.select(location=loc)\n\n            if len(station_stream) > 0:\n                # Get prediction from model\n                pred = model.predict(station_stream)\n\n                # Extract pick information\n                if pred is not None and 'P' in pred:\n                    p_phase = pred['P']\n                    s_phase = pred['S']\n\n                    for trace in station_stream:\n                        chan = trace.stats.channel\n                        if chan.endswith('Z'):  # Use vertical component\n                            for phase_type, phase_data in [('P', p_phase), ('S', s_phase)]:\n                                if phase_data is not None:\n                                    peak_idx = np.argmax(phase_data)\n                                    if phase_data[peak_idx] > 0.1:\n                                        pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                                        picks.append({\n                                            'network': net,\n                                            'station': sta,\n                                            'location': loc,\n                                            'channel': chan,\n                                            'phase': phase_type,\n                                            'time': pick_time,\n                                            'confidence': float(phase_data[peak_idx])\n                                        })\n                            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Error processing station: {e}\")\n\n    return picks\n\ndef pick_phases_simple(stream):\n    \"\"\"Simple phase picking based on energy detection.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Using simple energy-based phase picking...\")\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Group traces by station\n    stations = {}\n    for trace in stream:\n        sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station, trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n        if sta_key not in stations:\n            stations[sta_key] = []\n        stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n    # For each station, find picks from vertical component\n    for sta_key, station_traces in stations.items():\n        net, sta, loc = sta_key\n\n        # Find vertical component trace\n        z_trace = None\n        for trace in station_traces:\n            if trace.stats.channel.endswith('Z'):\n                z_trace = trace\n                break\n\n        if z_trace is None:\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate short-term average / long-term average (STA/LTA) ratio\n        data = z_trace.data.astype(float)\n\n        # Simple high-pass filter (remove DC and very low frequencies)\n        from scipy import signal\n        b, a = signal.butter(4, 0.05, btype='high')\n        filtered = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n\n        # Calculate energy\n        energy = np.abs(filtered)\n\n        # STA/LTA algorithm\n        lta_len = int(2 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 2 seconds\n        sta_len = int(0.5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n\n        lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n        sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n\n        ratio = np.divide(sta, lta, where=lta > 0, out=np.zeros_like(sta))\n\n        # Find peaks in the ratio (potential phase arrivals)\n        threshold = 2.5\n        peaks = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n        if len(peaks) > 0:\n            # Group nearby peaks\n            grouped_peaks = []\n            current_group = [peaks[0]]\n\n            for i in range(1, len(peaks)):\n                if peaks[i] - peaks[i-1] < z_trace.stats.sampling_rate:  # within 1 second\n                    current_group.append(peaks[i])\n                else:\n                    grouped_peaks.append(int(np.mean(current_group)))\n                    current_group = [peaks[i]]\n            grouped_peaks.append(int(np.mean(current_group)))\n\n            # Extract picks (P and S if we have multiple peaks)\n            for phase_idx, peak in enumerate(grouped_peaks[:2]):  # Use first 2 peaks as P and S\n                phase_type = 'P' if phase_idx == 0 else 'S'\n                pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + peak * z_trace.stats.delta\n                picks.append({\n                    'network': net,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'location': loc,\n                    'channel': z_trace.stats.channel,\n                    'phase': phase_type,\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'confidence': float(ratio[peak])\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_picks(picks, station_coords):\n    \"\"\"Associate picks into earthquake events using simple clustering.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nAssociating {len(picks)} picks into events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return []\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Convert time to seconds for clustering\n    picks_df['time_sec'] = picks_df['time'].apply(lambda t: (t - picks_df['time'].min()) * 1000)\n\n    # Convert time values to seconds from first pick\n    pick_times = np.array([float((t - picks_df['time'].min())) for t in picks_df['time']])\n\n    # Simple hierarchical clustering based on time\n    # Events should have picks within a few seconds of each other\n    events = []\n\n    if len(pick_times) > 0:\n        # Cluster picks by time\n        time_threshold = 10.0  # seconds - picks within 10 seconds are likely same event\n\n        # Sort by time\n        sorted_indices = np.argsort(pick_times)\n\n        current_event = [sorted_indices[0]]\n\n        for i in range(1, len(sorted_indices)):\n            idx = sorted_indices[i]\n            last_idx = current_event[-1]\n\n            if abs(pick_times[idx] - pick_times[last_idx]) < time_threshold:\n                current_event.append(idx)\n            else:\n                # Start new event\n                if len(current_event) >= 3:  # Need at least 3 picks for an event\n                    events.append(current_event)\n                current_event = [idx]\n\n        if len(current_event) >= 3:\n            events.append(current_event)\n\n    # Convert event indices to event information\n    event_list = []\n    for event_picks_indices in events:\n        event_picks = picks_df.iloc[event_picks_indices]\n\n        # Event time is the median of P-wave arrivals\n        p_picks = event_picks[event_picks['phase'] == 'P']\n        if len(p_picks) > 0:\n            event_time = p_picks['time'].median()\n        else:\n            event_time = event_picks['time'].min()\n\n        event_list.append({\n            'time': event_time,\n            'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n            'num_stations': len(event_picks['station'].unique())\n        })\n\n    return event_list\n\ndef write_results(events, output_file):\n    \"\"\"Write event results to CSV file.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to {output_file}...\")\n\n    if not events:\n        # Create empty results file\n        pd.DataFrame(columns=['time']).to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n        return\n\n    # Convert to DataFrame\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n\n    # Format time as ISO format without timezone\n    results_df['time'] = results_df['time'].apply(\n        lambda t: t.datetime.isoformat() if hasattr(t, 'datetime') else str(t)\n    )\n\n    # Select columns\n    output_df = results_df[['time']].copy()\n    if 'num_picks' in results_df.columns:\n        output_df['num_picks'] = results_df['num_picks']\n    if 'num_stations' in results_df.columns:\n        output_df['num_stations'] = results_df['num_stations']\n\n    output_df.to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n    print(f\"Results written to {output_file}\")\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    #                  for phase_idx, phase_type in enumerate(['P', 'S']):\n                                if phase_idx < len(pred):\n                                    phase_probs = pred[phase_idx]\n                                    peak_idx = np.argmax(phase_probs)\n                                    peak_prob = phase_probs[peak_idx]\n\n                                    if peak_prob > 0.5:  # station\n        stations = {}\n        for trace in stream:\n            sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station,\n                      trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n            if sta_key not in stations:\n                stations[sta_key] = []\n            stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n        for sta_key, traces in stations.items():\n            net,      picks.append({\n                                    'network': net,\n                                    'station': sta,\n                                    'location': loc,\n                                    'channel': 'HHZ',\n                                    'phase': 'P',\n                                    'time': station_stream[0].stats.starttime + p_time,\n                                    'confidence': 0.8\n                                })\n\n                    if 'S_picks' in predictions:\n                        for station_idx, s_time in                                 'station': sta,\n                                    'location': loc,\n                                    'channel': 'HHZ',\n                                    'phase': 'S',\n                                    'time': station_stream[0].stats.starttime + s_time,\n                                    'confidence': 0.8\n                                })\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"    Error processing station: {e}\")\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with EQTransformer: {e}\")\n        return None\n\ndef pick_phases_fallback(stream):\n    \"\"\"Fallback phase picking - using simple STA/LTA detection.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Using fallback STA/LTA phase picking...\")\n\n    from scipy import signal\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Group by station\n    stations = {}\n    for trace in stream:\n        sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station,\n                  trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n        if sta_key not in stations:\n            stations[sta_key] = []\n        stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n    for sta_key, traces in stations.items():\n        net, sta, loc = sta_key\n\n        # Use vertical component\n        z_trace = None\n        for trace in traces:\n            if trace.stats.channel.endswith('Z'):\n                z_trace = trace\n                break\n\n        if z_trace is None:\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"  Processing {net}.{sta}.{loc}\")\n\n        # Preprocess data\n        data = z_trace.data.astype(float)\n\n        # High-pass filter\n        nyquist = z_trace.stats.sampling_rate / 2\n        normalized_freq = 0.5 / nyquist\n        if normalized_freq < 1.0:\n            b, a = signal.butter(4, normalized_freq, btype='high')\n            data = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n\n        # Calculate energy\n        energy = np.square(data)\n\n        # STA/LTA\n        lta_len = int(5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n        sta_len = int(0.5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n\n        lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n        sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n\n        # Avoid division by zero\n        ratio = np.divide(sta, lta, where=lta > 1e-10, out=np.zeros_like(sta))\n\n        # Find threshold crossings\n        threshold = 2.0\n        above_threshold = ratio > threshold\n\n        # Find peaks/trigger points\n        if np.any(above_threshold):\n            # Find start and end points of threshold violations\n            diff = np.diff(above_threshold.astype(int))\n            starts = np.where(diff == 1)[0] + 1\n            ends = np.where(diff == -1)[0]\n\n            # Extract peaks from each triggered segment\n            picked_times = []\n            min_distance = int(0.5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds minimum between picks\n\n            for start, end in zip(starts, ends):\n                if end - start > 10:  # At least 10 samples\n                    segment = ratio[start:end]\n                    peak_idx_in_segment = np.argmax(segment)\n                    peak_idx = start + peak_idx_in_segment\n\n                    # Check if we're far enough from previous picks\n                    if not picked_times or (peak_idx - picked_times[-1]) > min_distance:\n                        picked_times.append(peak_idx)\n\n            # Assign P and S waves\n            for phase_idx, peak_idx in enumerate(picked_times[:2]):  # First 2 peaks are P and S\n                phase_type = 'P' if phase_idx == 0 else 'S'\n                pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * z_trace.stats.delta\n                picks.append({\n                    'network': net,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'location': loc,\n                    'channel': z_trace.stats.channel,\n                    'phase': phase_type,\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'confidence': float(ratio[peak_idx])\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_picks_gamma(picks):\n    \"\"\"Associate picks using a simplified GaMMA-like approach.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nAssociating {len(picks)} picks into events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return []\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Group picks by approximate time (within 15 seconds)\n    time_threshold = 15.0  # seconds\n\n    # Convert times to seconds from start\n    first_time = picks_df['time'].min()\n    picks_df['time_offset'] = (picks_df['time'] - first_time).dt.total_seconds()\n\n    # Sort by time\n    picks_df = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /etc/claude-code/.claude/skills/seisbench-model-api\n\n# SeisBench Model API\n\n## Installing SeisBench\nThe recommended way is installation through pip. Simply run:\n```\npip install seisbench\n```\n\n## Overview\n\nSeisBench offers the abstract class `WaveformModel` that every SeisBench model should subclass. This class offers two core functions, `annotate` and `classify`. Both of the functions are automatically generated based on configurations and submethods implemented in the specific model.\n\nThe `SeisBenchModel` bridges the gap between the pytorch interface of the models and the obspy interface common in seismology. It automatically assembles obspy streams into pytorch tensors and reassembles the results into streams. It also takes care of batch processing. Computations can be run on GPU by simply moving the model to GPU.\n\nThe `annotate` function takes an obspy stream object as input and returns annotations as stream again. For example, for picking models the output would be the characteristic functions, i.e., the pick probabilities over time.\n\n```python\nstream = obspy.read(\"my_waveforms.mseed\")\nannotations = model.annotate(stream)  # Returns obspy stream object with annotations\n```\n\nThe `classify` function also takes an obspy stream as input, but in contrast to the `annotate` function returns discrete results. The structure of these results might be model dependent. For example, a pure picking model will return a list of picks, while a picking and detection model might return a list of picks and a list of detections.\n\n```python\nstream = obspy.read(\"my_waveforms.mseed\")\noutputs = model.classify(stream)  # Returns a list of picks\nprint(outputs)\n```\n\nBoth `annotate` and `classify` can be supplied with waveforms from multiple stations at once and will automatically handle the correct grouping of the traces. For details on how to build your own model with SeisBench, check the documentation of `WaveformModel`. For details on how to apply models, check out the Examples.\n\n## Loading Pretrained Models\n\nFor annotating waveforms in a meaningful way, trained model weights are required. SeisBench offers a range of pretrained model weights through a common interface. Model weights are downloaded on the first use and cached locally afterwards. For some model weights, multiple versions are available. For details on accessing these, check the documentation at `from_pretrained`.\n\n```python\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\n\nsbm.PhaseNet.list_pretrained()                  # Get available models\nmodel = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")  # Load the original model weights released by PhaseNet authors\n```\n\nPretrained models can not only be used for annotating data, but also offer a great starting point for transfer learning.\n\n## Speeding Up Model Application\n\nWhen applying models to large datasets, run time is often a major concern. Here are a few tips to make your model run faster:\n\n- **Run on GPU.** Execution on GPU is usually faster, even though exact speed-ups vary between models. However, we note that running on GPU is not necessarily the most economic option. For example, in cloud applications it might be cheaper (and equally fast) to pay for a handful of CPU machines to annotate a large dataset than for a GPU machine.\n\n- **Use a large `batch_size`.** This parameter can be passed as an optional argument to all models. Especially on GPUs, larger batch sizes lead to faster annotations. As long as the batch fits into (GPU) memory, it might be worth increasing the batch size.\n\n- **Compile your model (torch 2.0+).** If you are using torch in version 2.0 or newer, compile your model. It's as simple as running `model = torch.compile(model)`. The compilation will take some time but if you are annotating large amounts of waveforms, it should pay off quickly. Note that there are many options for compile that might influence the performance gains considerably.\n\n- **Use asyncio interface.** Load data in parallel while executing the model using the asyncio interface, i.e., `annotate_asyncio` and `classify_asyncio`. This is usually substantially faster because data loading is IO-bound while the actual annotation is compute-bound.\n\n- **Manual resampling.** While SeisBench can automatically resample the waveforms, it can be faster to do the resampling manually beforehand. SeisBench uses obspy routines for resampling, which (as of 2023) are not parallelised. Check the required sampling rate with `model.sampling_rate`. Alternative routines are available, e.g., in the Pyrocko library.\n\n## Models Integrated into SeisBench\n\nYou don't have to build models from scratch if you don't want to. SeisBench integrates the following notable models from the literature for you to use. Again, as they inherit from the common SeisBench model interface, all these deep learning models are constructed through PyTorch. Where possible, the original trained weights are imported and made available. These can be accessed via the `from_pretrained` method.\n\n| Integrated Model | Task |\n|------------------|------|\n| `BasicPhaseAE` | Phase Picking |\n| `CRED` | Earthquake Detection |\n| `DPP` | Phase Picking |\n| `DepthPhaseNet` | Depth estimation from depth phases |\n| `DepthPhaseTEAM` | Depth estimation from depth phases |\n| `DeepDenoiser` | Denoising |\n| `SeisDAE` | Denoising |\n| `EQTransformer` | Earthquake Detection/Phase Picking |\n| `GPD` | Phase Picking |\n| `LFEDetect` | Phase Picking (Low-frequency earthquakes) |\n| `OBSTransformer` | Earthquake Detection/Phase Picking |\n| `PhaseNet` | Phase Picking |\n| `PhaseNetLight` | Phase Picking |\n| `PickBlue` | Earthquake Detection/Phase Picking |\n| `Skynet` | Phase Picking |\n| `VariableLengthPhaseNet` | Phase Picking |\n\nCurrently integrated models are capable of earthquake detection and phase picking, waveform denoising, depth estimation, and low-frequency earthquake phase picking. Furthermore, with SeisBench you can build ML models to perform general seismic tasks such as magnitude and source parameter estimation, hypocentre determination etc.\n\n## Best Practices\n- If the waveform data happen to be extremely small in scale (`<=1e-10`), there might be risk of numerical instability. It is acceptable to increase the value first (by multiplying a large number like `1e10`) before normalization or passing to the model.\n\n- Although the seisbench model API will normalize the waveform for you, it is still highly suggested to apply normalization yourself. Since seisbench's normalization scheme uses an epsilon `(waveform - mean(waveform)) / (std(waveform) + epsilon)`, for extremely small values (such as `<=1e-10`), their normalization can destroy the signals in the waveform.\n\n- The seisbench model API can process a stream of waveform data of arbitrary length. Hence, it is not necessary to segment the data yourself. In addition, you should not assume a stream of waveform can only contain one P-wave and one S-wave. It is the best to treat the stream like what it is: a stream of continuous data.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff0c55a3-9f30-483f-b584-1712be2dad68","uuid":"41010b81-8d27-41dc-a899-1a0d1bc5fb51","isSynthetic":true}

   picks = []\n        if predictions is not None:\n            print(f\"Got predictions: {type(predictions)}\")\n            for pred in predictions:\n                print(f\"Prediction: {pred}\")\n                picks.append(pred)\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with EQTransformer: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef pick_phases_annotate(stream):\n    \"\"\"Use annotate method to get characteristic functions.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        import seisbench.models as sbm\n\n        print(\"Loading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\n        model = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n        print(\"Annotating stream to get characteristic functions...\")\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream)\n\n        print(f\"Got {len(annotations)} annotation traces\")\n        print(\"Annotation traces:\")\n        for trace in annotations:\n            print(f\"  {trace.stats.network}.{trace.stats.station}.{trace.stats.channel}: \"\n                  f\"{len(trace.data)} samples\")\n\n        picks = []\n\n        # Extract picks from annotations\n        # Annotations typically have channels like 'PN', 'SN' (Noise), 'PP', 'PS', 'SP', 'SS'\n        # or 'P_prob', 'S_prob'\n\n        # Group by station\n        stations = {}\n        for trace in annotations:\n            sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station,\n                      trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n            if sta_key not in stations:\n                stations[sta_key] = {}\n            channel = trace.stats.channel\n            stations[sta_key][channel] = trace\n\n        # Process each station\n        for (net, sta, loc), channels_dict in stations.items():\n            print(f\"Processing picks for {net}.{sta}.{loc}\")\n            print(f\"  Available channels: {list(channels_dict.keys())}\")\n\n            # Look for P and S picks\n            for phase_type, channel_prefix in [('P', 'P'), ('S', 'S')]:\n                # Find matching channel (e.g., 'PN' or 'PP')\n                matching_channels = [ch for ch in channels_dict.keys() if ch.startswith(channel_prefix)]\n\n                for channel in matching_channels:\n                    trace = channels_dict[channel]\n                    probs = trace.data\n\n                    # Find the peak\n                    peak_idx = np.argmax(probs)\n                    peak_prob = probs[peak_idx]\n\n                    if peak_prob > 0.1:  # Confidence threshold\n                        pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                        picks.append({\n                            'network': net,\n                            'station': sta,\n                            'location': loc,\n                            'channel': channel,\n                            'phase': phase_type,\n                            'time': pick_time,\n                            'confidence': float(peak_prob)\n                        })\n\n                        print(f\"    Found {phase_type} pick in {channel} at {pick_time} (prob={peak_prob:.3f})\")\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with annotation method: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef pick_phases_fallback(stream):\n    \"\"\"Fallback phase picking - using simple energy-based detection.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Using energy-based phase picking...\")\n\n    from scipy import signal\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Group by station\n    stations = {}\n    for trace in stream:\n        sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station,\n                  trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n        if sta_key not in stations:\n            stations[sta_key] = []\n        stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n    for sta_key, traces in stations.items():\n        net, sta, loc = sta_key\n\n        # Use vertical component\n        z_trace = None\n        for trace in traces:\n            if trace.stats.channel.endswith('Z'):\n                z_trace = trace\n                break\n\n        if z_trace is None:\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"  Processing {net}.{sta}.{loc}\")\n\n        # Preprocess data\n        data = z_trace.data.astype(float)\n\n        # High-pass filter\n        nyquist = z_trace.stats.sampling_rate / 2\n        normalized_freq = 0.5 / nyquist\n        if normalized_freq < 1.0:\n            b, a = signal.butter(4, normalized_freq, btype='high')\n            data = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n\n        # Calculate energy\n        energy = np.square(data)\n\n        # STA/LTA\n        lta_len = int(5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n        sta_len = int(0.5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n\n        lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n        sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n\n        # Avoid division by zero\n        ratio = np.divide(sta, lta, where=lta > 1e-10, out=np.zeros_like(sta))\n\n        # Find threshold crossings\n        threshold = 2.0\n        above_threshold = ratio > threshold\n\n        # Find peaks\n        if np.any(above_threshold):\n            diff = np.diff(above_threshold.astype(int))\n            starts = np.where(diff == 1)[0] + 1\n            ends = np.where(diff == -1)[0]\n\n            picked_times = []\n            min_distance = int(0.5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)\n\n            for start, end in zip(starts, ends):\n                if end - start > 10:\n                    segment = ratio[start:end]\n                    peak_idx_in_segment = np.argmax(segment)\n                    peak_idx = start + peak_idx_in_segment\n\n                    if not picked_times or (peak_idx - picked_times[-1]) > min_distance:\n                        picked_times.append(peak_idx)\n\n            # Assign P and S waves\n            for phase_idx, peak_idx in enumerate(picked_times[:2]):\n                phase_type = 'P' if phase_idx == 0 else 'S'\n                pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * z_trace.stats.delta\n                picks.append({\n                    'network': net,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'location': loc,\n                    'channel': z_trace.stats.channel,\n                    'phase': phase_type,\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'confidence': float(ratio[peak_idx])\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_picks(picks):\n channels_dict:\n                    trace = channels_dict[channel_name]\n                    probs = trace.data\n\n                    # Find the peak\n                    peak_idx = np.argmax(probs)\n                    peak_prob = probs[peak_idx]\n\n                    if peak_prob > 0.1:  # Confidence threshold\n                        pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                        picks.append({\n                            'network': net,\n                            'station': sta,\n                            'location': loc,\n                            'channel': channel_name,\n                            'phase': phase_type,\n                            'time': pick_time,\n                            'confidence': float(peak_prob)\n                        })\n\n                        print(f\"    Found {phase_type} pick at {pick_time} (prob={peak_prob:.3f})\")\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with annotation method: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef pick_phases_fallback(stream):\n    \"\"\"Fallback phase picking - using simple energy-based detection.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Using energy-based phase picking...\")\n\n    from scipy import signal\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Group by station\n    stations = {}\n    for trace in stream:\n        sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station,\n                  trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n        if sta_key not in stations:\n            stations[sta_key] = []\n        stations[sta_key].append(trace)\n\n    for sta_key, traces in stations.items():\n        net, sta, loc = sta_key\n\n        # Use vertical component\n        z_trace = None\n        for trace in traces:\n            if trace.stats.channel.endswith('Z'):\n                z_trace = trace\n                break\n\n        if z_trace is None:\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"  Processing {net}.{sta}.{loc}\")\n\n        # Preprocess data\n        data = z_trace.data.astype(float)\n\n        # High-pass filter\n        nyquist = z_trace.stats.sampling_rate / 2\n        normalized_freq = 0.5 / nyquist\n        if normalized_freq < 1.0:\n            b, a = signal.butter(4, normalized_freq, btype='high')\n            data = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n\n        # Calculate energy\n        energy = np.square(data)\n\n        # STA/LTA\n        lta_len = int(5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 5 seconds\n        sta_len = int(0.5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)  # 0.5 seconds\n\n        lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n        sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n\n        # Avoid division by zero\n        ratio = np.divide(sta, lta, where=lta > 1e-10, out=np.zeros_like(sta))\n\n        # Find threshold crossings\n        threshold = 2.0\n        above_threshold = ratio > threshold\n\n        # Find peaks\n        if np.any(above_threshold):\n            diff = np.diff(above_threshold.astype(int))\n            starts = np.where(diff == 1)[0] + 1\n            ends = np.where(diff == -1)[0]\n\n            picked_times = []\n            min_distance = int(0.5 * z_trace.stats.sampling_rate)\n\n            for start, end in zip(starts, ends):\n                if end - start > 10:\n                    segment = ratio[start:end]\n                    peak_idx_in_segment = np.argmax(segment)\n                    peak_idx = start + peak_idx_in_segment\n\n                    if not picked_times or (peak_idx - picked_times[-1]) > min_distance:\n                        picked_times.append(peak_idx)\n\n            # Assign P and S waves\n            for phase_idx, peak_idx in enumerate(picked_times[:2]):\n                phase_type = 'P' if phase_idx == 0 else 'S'\n                pick_time = z_trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * z_trace.stats.delta\n                picks.append({\n                    'network': net,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'location': loc,\n                    'channel': z_trace.stats.channel,\n                    'phase': phase_type,\n                    'time': pick_time,\n                    'confidence': float(ratio[peak_idx])\n                })\n\n    return picks\n\ndef associate_picks(picks):\n    \"\"\"Associate picks into earthquake events.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nAssociating {len(picks)} picks into events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return []\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Group picks by approximate time (within 15 seconds)\n    time_threshold = 15.0  # seconds\n\n    # Convert times to seconds from start\n    first_time = picks_df['time'].min()\n    picks_df['time_offset'] = (picks_df['time'] - first_time).dt.total_seconds()\n\n    # Sort by time\n    picks_df = picks_df.sort_values('time_offset').reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    events = []\n    current_event = []\n    last_time = None\n\n    for idx, row in picks_df.iterrows():\n        if last_time is None:\n            current_event.append(idx)\n            last_time = row['time_offset']\n        elif row['time_offset'] - last_time < time_threshold:\n            current_event.append(idx)\n            last_time = row['time_offset']\n        else:\n            # Start new event\n            if len(current_event) >= 3:  # Need at least 3 picks for an event\n                events.append(current_event)\n            current_event = [idx]\n            last_time = row['time_offset']\n\n    # Don't forget the last event\n    if len(current_event) >= 3:\n        events.append(current_event)\n\n    # Convert to event list with times\n    event_list = []\n    for event_indices in events:\n        event_picks = picks_df.iloc[event_indices]\n\n        # Event time is the median of P-wave picks\n        p_picks = event_picks[event_picks['phase'] == 'P']\n        if len(p_picks) > 0:\n            event_time = p_picks['time'].median()\n        else:\n            event_time = event_picks['time'].min()\n\n        event_list.append({\n            'time': event_time,\n            'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n            'num_stations': len(event_picks['station'].unique()),\n            'phases': ','.join(event_picks['phase'].unique())\n        })\n\n    return event_list\n\ndef write_results(events, output_file):\n    \"\"\"Write  )\n\n    # Keep only time column for required output\n    output_df = results_df[['time']].copy()\n\n    # Add other columns for reference\n    if 'num_picks' in results_df.columns:\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_bdrk_018pTEMC477ofzJpMEWwejza","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_bdrk_01RksdUT7dQYFwGL9WQiEjTs","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/phase_association_v4.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSeismic phase association: identify earthquake events from waveforms and station data.\nUses SeisBench for phase picking and improved event association.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom obspy import read\nimport warnings\nfrom scipy.spatial.distance import pdist, squareform\nfrom scipy.cluster.hierarchy import linkage, fcluster\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Velocity model\nVP = 6.0  # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    stream = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n\n    print(\"Loading station metadata...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n\n    return stream, stations_df\n\ndef pick_phases_seisbench(stream):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using SeisBench.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        import seisbench.models as sbm\n\n        print(\"Loading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\n        model = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n        print(\"Annotating stream...\")\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream)\n\n        picks = []\n\n        # Group by station\n        stations = {}\n        for trace in annotations:\n            sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station,\n                      trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n            if sta_key not in stations:\n                stations[sta_key] = {}\n            channel = trace.stats.channel\n            stations[sta_key][channel] = trace\n\n        # Process each station\n        for (net, sta, loc), channels_dict in stations.items():\n            # Extract P and S picks from characteristic functions\n            for phase_type in ['P', 'S']:\n                channel_name = f'PhaseNet_{phase_type}'\n                if channel_name in channels_dict:\n                    trace = channels_dict[channel_name]\n                    probs = trace.data\n\n                    # Find peaks - look for all significant peaks, not just the maximum\n                    # Use a threshold to find potential picks\n                    threshold = 0.3\n                    peaks_indices = np.where(probs > threshold)[0]\n\n                    if len(peaks_indices) > 0:\n                        # Group consecutive indices\n                        groups = np.split(peaks_indices, np.where(np.diff(peaks_indices) > 10)[0] + 1)\n\n                        for group in groups:\n                            # Find the maximum within each group\n                            local_max_idx = group[np.argmax(probs[group])]\n                            peak_prob = probs[local_max_idx]\n\n                            if peak_prob > 0.3:  # Confidence threshold\n                                pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + local_max_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                                picks.append({\n                                    'network': net,\n                                    'station': sta,\n                                    'location': loc,\n                                    'channel': channel_name,\n                                    'phase': phase_type,\n                                    'time': pick_time,\n                                    'confidence': float(peak_prob)\n                                })\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with SeisBench: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef associate_picks_improved(picks):\n    \"\"\"Associate picks using travel time information.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nAssociating {len(picks)} picks into events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return []\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Convert times to seconds from first pick for easier manipulation\n    first_time = picks_df['time'].min()\n    picks_df['time_sec'] = picks_df['time'].apply(\n        lambda t: float((t - first_time).total_seconds())\n    )\n\n    # For event association, we use a simple approach:\n    # 1. Group picks that arrive within a maximum time window (considering P-S travel time)\n    # 2. For each group, check if picks come from different stations\n    # 3. Form events from coherent groups\n\n    events = []\n\n    # Sort by time\n    picks_df_sorted = picks_df.sort_values('time_sec').reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    # Use a rolling window approach\n    window_size = 20.0  # 20 seconds - this should capture most P and S arrivals from same event\n\n    used_indices = set()\n\n    for start_idx in range(len(picks_df_sorted)):\n        if start_idx in used_indices:\n            continue\n\n        current_pick = picks_df_sorted.iloc[start_idx]\n        event_picks = [start_idx]\n        used_indices.add(start_idx)\n\n        # Find all picks within the time window\n        for j in range(start_idx + 1, len(picks_df_sorted)):\n            if j in used_indices:\n                continue\n\n            other_pick = picks_df_sorted.iloc[j]\n            time_diff = other_pick['time_sec'] - current_pick['time_sec']\n\n            # If time difference is within window and from different station, add to event\n            if time_diff < window_size:\n                if other_pick['station'] != current_pick['station']:\n                    event_picks.append(j)\n                    used_indices.add(j)\n\n        # Only consider as an event if we have picks from at least 3 different stations\n        event_data = picks_df_sorted.iloc[event_picks]\n        unique_stations = event_data['station'].nunique()\n\n        if unique_stations >= 3 and len(event_picks) >= 3:\n            events.append(event_picks)\n\n    # Convert to event list with times\n    event_list = []\n    for i, event_indices in enumerate(events):\n        event_picks = picks_df_sorted.iloc[event_indices]\n\n        # Event time is the median of P-wave picks\n        p_picks = event_picks[event_picks['phase'] == 'P']\n        if len(p_picks) > 0:\n            # Use the first P pick as event time (more stable)\n            event_time = p_picks['time'].iloc[0]\n        else:\n            event_time = event_picks['time'].iloc[0]\n\n        event_list.append({\n            'time': event_time,\n            'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n            'num_stations': len(event_picks['station'].unique()),\n            'phases': ','.join(sorted(event_picks['phase'].unique())),\n            'event_id': i + 1\n        })\n\n    return event_list\n\ndef associate_picks_gamma_simple(picks):\n    \"\"\"Simplified GaMMA-like association.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nAssociating {len(picks)} picks into events using improved clustering...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return []\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Extract just P picks for clustering (more reliable)\n    p_picks = picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'].reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    if len(p_picks) < 3:\n        print(f\"Only {len(p_picks)} P picks, need at least 3 for events\")\n        return []\n\n    print(f\"Using {len(p_picks)} P picks for clustering\")\n\n    # Use find_peaks to locate significant peaks\n                    # With proper prominence and distance constraints\n                    peaks, properties = find_peaks(\n                        probs,\n                        height=0.4,  # High confidence threshold\n                            pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                            picks.append({\n                                'network': net,\n                                'station': sta,\n                                'location': loc,\n                                'channel': channel_name,\n                                'phase': phase_type,\n                                'time': pick_time,\n                                'confidence': float(peak_prob)\n                            })\n\n            if station_count % 5 == 0:\n                print(f\"  Processed {station_count} stations...\")\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with SeisBench: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef associate_picks(picks):\n    \"\"\"Associate picks into earthquake events.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nAssociating {len(picks)} picks into events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return []\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /etc/claude-code/.claude/skills/gamma-phase-associator\n\n# GaMMA Associator Library\n\n## What is GaMMA?\nGaMMA is an earthquake phase association algorithm that treats association as an unsupervised clustering problem. It uses multivariate Gaussian distribution to model the collection of phase picks of an event, and uses Expectation-Maximization to carry out pick assignment and estimate source parameters i.e., earthquake location, origin time, and magnitude.\n\n`GaMMA` is a python library implementing the algorithm. For the input earthquake traces, this library assumes P/S wave picks have already been extracted. We provide documentation of its core API.\n\n`Zhu, W., McBrearty, I. W., Mousavi, S. M., Ellsworth, W. L., & Beroza, G. C. (2022). Earthquake phase association using a Bayesian Gaussian mixture model. Journal of Geophysical Research: Solid Earth, 127(5).`\n\nThe skill is a derivative of the repo https://github.com/AI4EPS/GaMMA\n\n## Installing GaMMA\n`pip install git+https://github.com/wayneweiqiang/GaMMA.git`\n\n## GaMMA core API\n\n\n### `association`\n\n#### Function Signature\n\n```python\ndef association(picks, stations, config, event_idx0=0, method=\"BGMM\", **kwargs)\n```\n\n#### Purpose\n\nAssociates seismic phase picks (P and S waves) to earthquake events using Bayesian or standard Gaussian Mixture Models. It clusters picks based on arrival time and amplitude information, then fits GMMs to estimate earthquake locations, times, and magnitudes.\n\n#### 1. Input Parameters\n\n| Parameter | Type | Default | Description |\n|-----------|------|---------|-------------|\n| `picks` | DataFrame | required | Seismic phase pick data |\n| `stations` | DataFrame | required | Station metadata with locations |\n| `config` | dict | required | Configuration parameters |\n| `event_idx0` | int | `0` | Starting event index for numbering |\n| `method` | str | `\"BGMM\"` | `\"BGMM\"` (Bayesian) or `\"GMM\"` (standard) |\n\n#### 2. Required DataFrame Columns\n\n##### `picks` DataFrame\n\n| Column | Type | Description | Example |\n|--------|------|-------------|---------|\n| `id` | str | Station identifier (must match `stations`) | `network.station.` or `network.station.location.channel` |\n| `timestamp` | datetime/str | Pick arrival time (ISO format or datetime) | `\"2019-07-04T22:00:06.084\"` |\n| `type` | str | Phase type: `\"p\"` or `\"s\"` (lowercase) | `\"p\"` |\n| `prob` | float | Pick probability/weight (0-1) | `0.94` |\n| `amp` | float | Amplitude in m/s (required if `use_amplitude=True`) | `0.000017` |\n\n**Notes:**\n- Timestamps must be in UTC or converted to UTC\n- Phase types are forced to lowercase internally\n- Picks with `amp == 0` or `amp == -1` are filtered when `use_amplitude=True`\n- The DataFrame index is used to track pick identities in the output\n\n##### `stations` DataFrame\n\n| Column | Type | Description | Example |\n|--------|------|-------------|---------|\n| `id` | str | Station identifier | `\"CI.CCC..BH\"`|\n| `x(km)` | float | X coordinate in km (projected) | `-35.6` |\n| `y(km)` | float | Y coordinate in km (projected) | `45.2` |\n| `z(km)` | float | Z coordinate (elevation, typically negative) | `-0.67` |\n\n**Notes:**\n- Coordinates should be in a projected local coordinate system (e.g., you can use the `pyproj` package)\n- The `id` column must match the `id` values in the picks DataFrame (e.g., `network.station.` or `network.station.location.channel`)\n- Group stations by unique `id`, identical attribute are collapsed to a single value and conflicting metadata are preseved as a sorted list.  \n\n#### 3. Config Dictionary Keys\n\n##### Required Keys\n\n| Key | Type | Description | Example |\n|-----|------|-------------|---------|\n| `dims` | list[str] | Location dimensions to solve for | `[\"x(km)\", \"y(km)\", \"z(km)\"]` |\n| `min_picks_per_eq` | int | Minimum picks required per earthquake | `5` |\n| `max_sigma11` | float | Maximum allowed time residual in seconds | `2.0` |\n| `use_amplitude` | bool | Whether to use amplitude in clustering | `True` |\n| `bfgs_bounds` | tuple | Bounds for BFGS optimization | `((-35, 92), (-128, 78), (0, 21), (None, None))` |\n| `oversample_factor` | float | Factor for oversampling initial GMM components | `5.0` for `BGMM`, `1.0` for `GMM`|\n\n**Notes on `dims`:**\n- Options: `[\"x(km)\", \"y(km)\", \"z(km)\"]`, `[\"x(km)\", \"y(km)\"]`, or `[\"x(km)\"]`\n\n**Notes on `bfgs_bounds`:**\n- Format: `((x_min, x_max), (y_min, y_max), (z_min, z_max), (None, None))`\n- The last tuple is for time (unbounded)\n\n##### Velocity Model Keys\n\n| Key | Type | Default | Description |\n|-----|------|---------|-------------|\n| `vel` | dict | `{\"p\": 6.0, \"s\": 3.47}` | Uniform velocity model (km/s) |\n| `eikonal` | dict/None | `None` | 1D velocity model for travel times |\n\n##### DBSCAN Pre-clustering Keys (Optional)\n\n| Key | Type | Default | Description |\n|-----|------|---------|-------------|\n| `use_dbscan` | bool | `True` | Enable DBSCAN pre-clustering |\n| `dbscan_eps` | float | `25` | Max time between picks (seconds) |\n| `dbscan_min_samples` | int | `3` | Min samples in DBSCAN neighborhood |\n| `dbscan_min_cluster_size` | int | `500` | Min cluster size for hierarchical splitting |\n| `dbscan_max_time_space_ratio` | float | `10` | Max time/space ratio for splitting |\n\n- `dbscan_eps` is obtained from `estimate_eps` Function\n\n##### Filtering Keys (Optional)\n\n| Key | Type | Default | Description |\n|-----|------|-------------|\n| `max_sigma22` | float | `1.0` | Max phase amplitude residual in log scale (required if `use_amplitude=True`) |\n| `max_sigma12` | float | `1.0` | Max covariance |\n| `max_sigma11` | float | `2.0` | Max phase time residual (s) |\n| `min_p_picks_per_eq` | int | `0` | Min P-phase picks per event |\n| `min_s_picks_per_eq` | int | `0` |Min S-phase picks per event |\n| `min_stations` | int | `5` |Min unique stations per event |\n\n##### Other Optional Keys\n\n| Key | Type | Default | Description |\n|-----|------|---------|-------------|\n| `covariance_prior` | list[float] | auto | Prior for covariance `[time, amp]` |\n| `ncpu` | int | auto | Number of CPUs for parallel processing |\n\n#### 4. Return Values\n\nReturns a tuple `(events, assignments)`:\n\n##### `events` (list[dict])\n\nList of dictionaries, each representing an associated earthquake:\n\n| Key | Type | Description |\n|-----|------|-------------|\n| `time` | str | Origin time (ISO 8601 with milliseconds) |\n| `magnitude` | float | Estimated magnitude (999 if `use_amplitude=False`) |\n| `sigma_time` | float | Time uncertainty (seconds) |\n| `sigma_amp` | float | Amplitude uncertainty (log10 scale) |\n| `cov_time_amp` | float | Time-amplitude covariance |\n| `gamma_score` | float | Association quality score |\n| `num_picks` | int | Total picks assigned |\n| `num_p_picks` | int | P-phase picks assigned |\n| `num_s_picks` | int | S-phase picks assigned |\n| `event_index` | int | Unique event index |\n| `x(km)` | float | X coordinate of hypocenter |\n| `y(km)` | float | Y coordinate of hypocenter |\n| `z(km)` | float | Z coordinate (depth) |\n\n##### `assignments` (list[tuple])\n\nList of tuples `(pick_index, event_index, gamma_score)`:\n- `pick_index`: Index in the original `picks` DataFrame\n- `event_index`: Associated event index\n- `gamma_score`: Probability/confidence of assignment\n\n\n### `estimate_eps` Function Documentation\n\n#### Function Signature\n\n```python\ndef estimate_eps(stations, vp, sigma=2.0)\n```\n\n#### Purpose\n\nEstimates an appropriate DBSCAN epsilon (eps) parameter for clustering seismic phase picks based on station spacing. The eps parameter controls the maximum time distance between picks that should be considered neighbors in the DBSCAN clustering algorithm.\n\n#### 1. Input Parameters\n\n| Parameter | Type | Default | Description |\n|-----------|------|---------|-------------|\n| `stations` | DataFrame | required | Station metadata with 3D coordinates |\n| `vp` | float | required | P-wave velocity in km/s |\n| `sigma` | float | `2.0` | Number of standard deviations above the mean |\n\n#### 2. Required DataFrame Columns\n\n##### `stations` DataFrame\n\n| Column | Type | Description | Example |\n|--------|------|-------------|---------|\n| `x(km)` | float | X coordinate in km | `-35.6` |\n| `y(km)` | float | Y coordinate in km | `45.2` |\n| `z(km)` | float | Z coordinate in km | `-0.67` |\n\n#### 3. Return Value\n\n| Type | Description |\n|------|-------------|\n| float | Epsilon value in **seconds** for use with DBSCAN clustering |\n\n#### 4. Example Usage\n\n```python\nfrom gamma.utils import estimate_eps\n\n# Assuming stations DataFrame is already prepared with x(km), y(km), z(km) columns\nvp = 6.0  # P-wave velocity in km/s\n\n# Estimate eps automatically based on station spacing\neps = estimate_eps(stations, vp, sigma=2.0)\n\n# Use in config\nconfig = {\n    \"use_dbscan\": True,\n    \"dbscan_eps\": eps,  # or use estimate_eps(stations, config[\"vel\"][\"p\"])\n    \"dbscan_min_samples\": 3,\n    # ... other config options\n}\n```\n\n##### Typical Usage Pattern\n\n```python\nfrom gamma.utils import association, estimate_eps\n\n# Automatic eps estimation\nconfig[\"dbscan_eps\"] = estimate_eps(stations, config[\"vel\"][\"p\"])\n\n# Or manual override (common in practice)\nconfig[\"dbscan_eps\"] = 15  # seconds\n```\n\n#### 5. Practical Notes\n\n- In example notebooks, the function is often **commented out** in favor of hardcoded values (10-15 seconds)\n- Practitioners may prefer manual tuning for specific networks/regions\n- Typical output values range from **10-20 seconds** depending on station density\n- Useful when optimal eps is unknown or when working with new networks\n\n#### 6.
