km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    stream = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n\n    print(\"Loading station metadata...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n\n    return stream, stations_df\n\ndef pick_phases_seisbench(stream):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using SeisBench.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        import seisbench.models as sbm\n\n        print(\"Loading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\n        model = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n        print(\"Annotating stream...\")\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream)\n\n        picks = []\n\n        # Group by station\n        stations = {}\n        for trace in annotations:\n            sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station,\n                      trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n            if sta_key not in stations:\n                stations[sta_key] = {}\n            channel = trace.stats.channel\n            stations[sta_key][channel] = trace\n\n        # Process each station\n        for (net, sta, loc), channels_dict in stations.items():\n            # Extract P and S picks from characteristic functions\n            for phase_type in ['P', 'S']:\n                channel_name = f'PhaseNet_{phase_type}'\n                if channel_name in channels_dict:\n                    trace = channels_dict[channel_name]\n                    probs = trace.data\n\n                    # Use find_peaks to locate significant peaks\n                    peaks, properties = find_peaks(\n                        probs,\n                        height=0.4,  # Minimum peak height\n                        distance=int(2 * trace.stats.sampling_rate),  # Min 2 seconds between peaks\n                        prominence=0.2  # Minimum prominence\n                    )\n\n                    # Sort by probability and take top picks\n                    peak_probs = probs[peaks]\n                    sorted_indices = np.argsort(-peak_probs)[:5]  # Top 5 peaks\n\n                    for idx in sorted_indices:\n                        peak_idx = peaks[idx]\n                        peak_prob = probs[peak_idx]\n\n                        if peak_prob > 0.5:  # High confidence threshold\n                            pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                            picks.append({\n                                'network': net,\n                                'station': sta,\n                                'location': loc,\n                                'channel': channel_name,\n                                'phase': phase_type,\n                                'time': pick_time,\n                                'confidence': float(peak_prob)\n                            })\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with SeisBench: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef prepare_gamma_inputs(picks, stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Prepare inputs for GaMMA association.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing inputs for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return None, None\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Create picks DataFrame for GaMMA\n    gamma_picks = []\n    for idx, pick in picks_df.iterrows():\n        # Convert time to ISO format string\n        time_str = pick['time'].isoformat()\n\n        # Create station id (network.station.location.channel)\n        sta_id = f\"{pick['network']}.{pick['station']}.{pick['location']}.\"\n\n        gamma_picks.append({\n            'id': sta_id,\n            'timestamp': time_str,\n            'type': pick['phase'].lower(),  # GaMMA expects lowercase\n            'prob': pick['confidence'],\n            'amp': 0.0  # We don't have amplitude info\n        })\n\n    picks_for_gamma = pd.DataFrame(gamma_picks)\n\n    # Create stations DataFrame for GaMMA\n    # We need to project coordinates to km\n    stations_unique = stations_df.drop_duplicates(subset=['network', 'station'])\n\n    # Use a simple projection: treat lon/lat as x/y in km\n    # This is a rough approximation; in real seismology you'd use proper projections\n    # Convert lat/lon to km from reference point\n    ref_lat = stations_unique['latitude'].mean()\n    ref_lon = stations_unique['longitude'].mean()\n\n    stations_for_gamma = []\n    for idx, sta in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        # Simple km conversion (1 degree ~ 111 km, varies with latitude)\n        lat_km = (sta['latitude'] - ref_lat) * 111.0\n        lon_km = (sta['longitude'] - ref_lon) * 111.0 * np.cos(np.radians(ref_lat))\n\n        # Elevation to depth (negative is underground in seismic conventions)\n        depth_km = -sta['elevation_m'] / 1000.0\n\n        sta_id = f\"{sta['network']}.{sta['station']}..BH\"\n\n        stations_for_gamma.append({\n            'id': sta_id,\n            'x(km)': lon_km,\n            'y(km)': lat_km,\n            'z(km)': depth_km\n        })\n\n    stations_for_gamma = pd.DataFrame(stations_for_gamma)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(picks_for_gamma)} picks from {len(stations_for_gamma)} stations\")\n\n    return picks_for_gamma, stations_for_gamma\n\ndef associate_with_gamma(picks_gamma, stations_gamma):\n    \"\"\"Use GaMMA for event association.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        from gamma.utils import association, estimate_eps\n\n        print(\"\\nRunning GaMMA association...\")\n\n        # Estimate eps based on station spacing\n        eps = estimate_eps(stations_gamma, VP, sigma=2.0)\n        print(f\"Estimated DBSCAN eps: {eps:.2f} seconds\")\n\n        # Configure GaMMA\n        config = {\n            \"vel\": {\"p\": VP, \"s\": VS},\n            \"dims\": [\"x(km)\", \"y(km)\", \"z(km)\"],\n            \"use_dbscan\": True,\n            \"dbscan_eps\": eps,\n            \"dbscan_min_samples\": 3,\n            \"dbscan_min_cluster_size\": 500,\n            \"dbscan_max_time_space_ratio\": 10,\n            \"use_amplitude\": False,  # No amplitude data\n            \"min_picks_per_eq\": 3,\n            \"max_sigma11\": 2.0,  # Max time residual in seconds\n            \"bfgs_bounds\": None  # Let GaMMA set bounds\n        }\n\n        # Run association\n        events, assignments = association(picks_gamma, stations_gamma, config, method=\"BGMM\")\n\n        print(f\"Found {len(events)} events\")\n\n        # Convert events to output format\n        event_list = []\n        for evt in events:\n            event_list.append({\n                'time': evt['time'],\n                'num_picks': evt['num_picks'],\n                'num_stations': len(set([picks_gamma.iloc[a[0]]['id'] for a in assignments if a[1] == evt['event_index']])),\n                'magnitude': evt.get('magnitude', 0.0),\n                'gamma_score': evt.get('gamma_score', 0.0)\n            })\n\n        return event_list\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with GaMMA: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef write_results(events, output_file):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events) if events else 0} events to {output_file}...\")\n\n    if not events:\n        pd.DataFrame(columns=['time']).to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n        print(f\"Results written to {output_file}\")\n        return\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n\n    # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    stream = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n\n    print(\"Loading station metadata...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n\n    return stream, stations_df\n\ndef pick_phases_seisbench(stream):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using SeisBench.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        import seisbench.models as sbm\n\n        print(\"Loading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\n        model = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n        print(\"Annotating stream...\")\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream)\n\n        picks = []\n\n        # Group by station\n        stations = {}\n        for trace in annotations:\n            sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station,\n                      trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n            if sta_key not in stations:\n                stations[sta_key] = {}\n            channel = trace.stats.channel\n            stations[sta_key][channel] = trace\n\n        # Process each station\n        for (net, sta, loc), channels_dict in stations.items():\n            # Extract P and S picks from characteristic functions\n            for phase_type in ['P', 'S']:\n                channel_name = f'PhaseNet_{phase_type}'\n                if channel_name in channels_dict:\n                    trace = channels_dict[channel_name]\n                    probs = trace.data\n\n                    # Use find_peaks to locate significant peaks\n                    peaks, properties = find_peaks(\n                        probs,\n                        height=0.4,  # Minimum peak height\n                        distance=int(2 * trace.stats.sampling_rate),  # Min 2 seconds between peaks\n                        prominence=0.2  # Minimum prominence\n                    )\n\n                    # Sort by probability and take top picks\n                    peak_probs = probs[peaks]\n                    sorted_indices = np.argsort(-peak_probs)[:5]  # Top 5 peaks\n\n                    for idx in sorted_indices:\n                        peak_idx = peaks[idx]\n                        peak_prob = probs[peak_idx]\n\n                        if peak_prob > 0.5:  # High confidence threshold\n                            pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                            picks.append({\n                                'network': net,\n                                'station': sta,\n                                'location': loc,\n                                'channel': channel_name,\n                                'phase': phase_type,\n                                'time': pick_time,\n                                'confidence': float(peak_prob)\n                            })\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with SeisBench: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef prepare_gamma_inputs(picks, stations_df):\n    \"\"\"Prepare inputs for GaMMA association.\"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nPreparing inputs for GaMMA...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return None, None\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Create picks DataFrame for GaMMA\n    gamma_picks = []\n    for idx, pick in picks_df.iterrows():\n        # Convert time to ISO format string (ensure it's a string, not datetime)\n        if hasattr(pick['time'], 'isoformat'):\n            time_str = pick['time'].isoformat()\n        else:\n            time_str = str(pick['time'])\n\n        # Create station id (network.station.location.channel)\n        sta_id = f\"{pick['network']}.{pick['station']}.{pick['location']}.\"\n\n        gamma_picks.append({\n            'id': str(sta_id),\n            'timestamp': str(time_str),  # Ensure it's a string\n            'type': str(pick['phase'].lower()),  # GaMMA expects lowercase\n            'prob': float(pick['confidence']),\n            'amp': 0.0  # We don't have amplitude info\n        })\n\n    picks_for_gamma = pd.DataFrame(gamma_picks)\n    # Ensure all columns are proper types\n    picks_for_gamma['id'] = picks_for_gamma['id'].astype(str)\n    picks_for_gamma['timestamp'] = picks_for_gamma['timestamp'].astype(str)\n    picks_for_gamma['type'] = picks_for_gamma['type'].astype(str)\n\n    # Create stations DataFrame for GaMMA\n    # We need to project coordinates to km\n    stations_unique = stations_df.drop_duplicates(subset=['network', 'station'])\n\n    # Use a simple projection: treat lon/lat as x/y in km\n    # This is a rough approximation; in real seismology you'd use proper projections\n    # Convert lat/lon to km from reference point\n    ref_lat = stations_unique['latitude'].mean()\n    ref_lon = stations_unique['longitude'].mean()\n\n    stations_for_gamma = []\n    for idx, sta in stations_unique.iterrows():\n        # Simple km conversion (1 degree ~ 111 km, varies with latitude)\n        lat_km = (sta['latitude'] - ref_lat) * 111.0\n        lon_km = (sta['longitude'] - ref_lon) * 111.0 * np.cos(np.radians(ref_lat))\n\n        # Elevation to depth (negative is underground in seismic conventions)\n        depth_km = -sta['elevation_m'] / 1000.0\n\n        sta_id = f\"{sta['network']}.{sta['station']}..BH\"\n\n        stations_for_gamma.append({\n            'id': sta_id,\n            'x(km)': lon_km,\n            'y(km)': lat_km,\n            'z(km)': depth_km\n        })\n\n    stations_for_gamma = pd.DataFrame(stations_for_gamma)\n\n    print(f\"Prepared {len(picks_for_gamma)} picks from {len(stations_for_gamma)} stations\")\n\n    return picks_for_gamma, stations_for_gamma\n\ndef associate_with_gamma(picks_gamma, stations_gamma):\n    \"\"\"Use GaMMA for event association.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        from gamma.utils import association, estimate_eps\n\n        print(\"\\nRunning GaMMA association...\")\n\n        # Estimate eps based on station spacing\n        eps = estimate_eps(stations_gamma, VP, sigma=2.0)\n        print(f\"Estimated DBSCAN eps: {eps:.2f} seconds\")\n\n        # Configure GaMMA\n        config = {\n            \"vel\": {\"p\": VP, \"s\": VS},\n            \"dims\": [\"x(km)\", \"y(km)\", \"z(km)\"],\n            \"use_dbscan\": True,\n            \"dbscan_eps\": eps,\n            \"dbscan_min_samples\": 3,\n            \"dbscan_min_cluster_size\": 500,\n            \"dbscan_max_time_space_ratio\": 10,\n            \"use_amplitude\": False,  # No amplitude data\n            \"min_picks_per_eq\": 3,\n            \"max_sigma11\": 2.0,  # Max time residual in seconds\n            \"bfgs_bounds\": None  # Let GaMMA set bounds\n        }\n\n        # Run association\n        events, assignments = association(picks_gamma, stations_gamma, config, method=\"BGMM\")\n\n        print(f\"Found {len(events)} events\")\n\n        # Convert events to output format\n        event_list = []\n        for evt in events:\n            event_list.append({\n                'time': evt['time'],\n                'num_picks': evt['num_picks'],\n                'num_stations': len(set([picks_gamma.iloc[a[0]]['id'] for a in assignments if a[1] == evt['event_index']])),\n                'magnitude': evt.get('magnitude', 0.0),\n                'gamma_score': evt.get('gamma_score', 0.0)\n            })\n\n        return event_list\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with GaMMA: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef write_results(events, output_file):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events) if events else 0} events to {output_file}...\")\n\n    if not events:\n        pd.DataFrame(columns=['time']).to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n        print(f\"Results written to {output_file}\")\n        return\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n\n    # Add other columns for reference\n    for col in ['num_picks', 'num_stations', 'magnitude', 'gamma_score']:\n        if col in results_df.columns:\n            output_df[col] = results_df[col]\n\n    output_df.to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n    print(f\"Results written to {output_file}\")\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"SEISMIC PHASE ASSOCIATION WITH SEISBENCH + GAMMA\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Use find_peaks to locate significant peaks\n                    # More aggressive constraints to get real events\n                    peaks, properties = find_peaks(\n                        probs,\n                        height=0.35,  # Minimum peak height\n                        distance=int(1.5 * trace.stats.sampling_rate),  # Min 1.5 seconds between peaks\n                        prominence=0.15  # Minimum prominence\n                    )\n\n                    # Convert times to numeric\n    picks_df['time_numeric'] = picks_df['time'].apply(lambda t: float(t.timestamp))\n    first_time = picks_df['time_numeric'].min()\n    picks_df['time_offset'] = (picks_df['time_numeric'] - first_time) / 1.0  # in seconds\n\n    # P picks are more reliable for clustering\n    p_picks = picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'].copy().reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    if len(p_picks) < 3:\n        print(f\"Only {len(p_picks)} P picks found\")\n        return []\n\n    print(f\"Using {len(p_picks)} P picks for clustering\")\n\n    # Cut dendrogram at 15 seconds\n    time_threshold = 15.0\n    clusters = fcluster(linkage_matrix, time_threshold, criterion='distance')\n\n    # For each cluster, check if picks are from different stations\n    events = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_mask = clusters == cluster_id\n        cluster_picks = p_picks[cluster_mask]\n\n        unique_stations = cluster_picks['station'].nunique()\n\n        # Remove duplicate/very close events (within 3 seconds)\n    if len(events) > 1:\n        events_sorted = sorted(events, key=lambda e: float(e['time'].timestamp))\n        unique_events = [events_sorted[0]]\n\n        for evt in events_sorted[1:]:\n            last_evt = unique_events[-1]\n            time_diff = float(evt['time'].timestamp) - float(last_evt['time'].timestamp)\n\n            # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load waveform and station data.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading waveform data...\")\n    stream = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\n\n    print(\"Loading station metadata...\")\n    stations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\n\n    return stream, stations_df\n\ndef pick_phases_optimized(stream):\n    \"\"\"Pick P and S waves using SeisBench with optimized peak detection.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        import seisbench.models as sbm\n\n        print(\"Loading SeisBench PhaseNet model...\")\n        model = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n        print(\"Annotating stream...\")\n        annotations = model.annotate(stream)\n\n        picks = []\n\n        # Group by station\n        stations = {}\n        for trace in annotations:\n            sta_key = (trace.stats.network, trace.stats.station,\n                      trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else '')\n            if sta_key not in stations:\n                stations[sta_key] = {}\n            channel = trace.stats.channel\n            stations[sta_key][channel] = trace\n\n        # Process each station\n        for (net, sta, loc), channels_dict in stations.items():\n            # Extract P and S picks from characteristic functions\n            for phase_type in ['P', 'S']:\n                channel_name = f'PhaseNet_{phase_type}'\n                if channel_name in channels_dict:\n                    trace = channels_dict[channel_name]\n                    probs = trace.data\n\n                    # Use find_peaks to locate significant peaks\n                    # More aggressive constraints to get real events\n                    peaks, properties = find_peaks(\n                        probs,\n                        height=0.35,  # Minimum peak height\n                        distance=int(1.5 * trace.stats.sampling_rate),  # Min 1.5 seconds between peaks\n                        prominence=0.15  # Minimum prominence\n                    )\n\n                    # Sort by probability and take top 8 peaks (some may be noise)\n                    if len(peaks) > 0:\n                        peak_probs = probs[peaks]\n                        sorted_indices = np.argsort(-peak_probs)[:8]\n\n                        for idx in sorted_indices:\n                            peak_idx = peaks[idx]\n                            peak_prob = probs[peak_idx]\n\n                            if peak_prob > 0.45:  # High confidence threshold\n                                pick_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx * trace.stats.delta\n                                picks.append({\n                                    'network': net,\n                                    'station': sta,\n                                    'location': loc,\n                                    'channel': channel_name,\n                                    'phase': phase_type,\n                                    'time': pick_time,\n                                    'confidence': float(peak_prob)\n                                })\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error with SeisBench: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return None\n\ndef associate_picks_hierarchical(picks):\n    \"\"\"Associate picks using hierarchical clustering on time and station.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nAssociating {len(picks)} picks into events...\")\n\n    if not picks:\n        return []\n\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Convert times to numeric\n    picks_df['time_numeric'] = picks_df['time'].apply(lambda t: float(t.timestamp))\n    first_time = picks_df['time_numeric'].min()\n    picks_df['time_offset'] = (picks_df['time_numeric'] - first_time) / 1.0  # in seconds\n\n    # P picks are more reliable for clustering\n    p_picks = picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'].copy().reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    if len(p_picks) < 3:\n        print(f\"Only {len(p_picks)} P picks found\")\n        return []\n\n    print(f\"Using {len(p_picks)} P picks for clustering\")\n\n    # Extract features for clustering\n    times = p_picks['time_offset'].values.reshape(-1, 1)\n\n    # Perform hierarchical clustering on time\n    # Distance threshold controls event separation\n    # 15 seconds should capture most P and S arrivals from same event\n    distances = squareform(pdist(times))\n    linkage_matrix = linkage(distances, method='complete')\n\n    # Cut dendrogram at 15 seconds\n    time_threshold = 15.0\n    clusters = fcluster(linkage_matrix, time_threshold, criterion='distance')\n\n    # For each cluster, check if picks are from different stations\n    events = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_mask = clusters == cluster_id\n        cluster_picks = p_picks[cluster_mask]\n\n        unique_stations = cluster_picks['station'].nunique()\n\n        # Only form event if we have picks from at least 3 different stations\n        if unique_stations >= 3 and len(cluster_picks) >= 3:\n            # Find S picks associated with this event\n            cluster_time_range = (cluster_picks['time_offset'].min(), cluster_picks['time_offset'].max())\n            first_pick_time = cluster_picks['time'].iloc[0]\n\n            # Find S picks within reasonable time range (S arrives after P)\n            s_picks_mask = picks_df['phase'] == 'S'\n            s_picks_for_event = []\n\n            for idx, s_pick in picks_df[s_picks_mask].iterrows():\n                s_time_offset = s_pick['time_offset']\n                # S should arrive between first P and +50 seconds after first P\n                if (cluster_time_range[0] <= s_time_offset <= cluster_time_range[0] + 50):\n                    s_picks_for_event.append(s_pick)\n\n            total_picks = len(cluster_picks) + len(s_picks_for_event)\n\n            if total_picks >= 3:\n                events.append({\n                    'time': first_pick_time,\n                    'num_picks': total_picks,\n                    'num_stations': unique_stations,\n                    'p_picks': len(cluster_picks),\n                    's_picks': len(s_picks_for_event)\n                })\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(events)} events\")\n\n    # Remove duplicate/very close events (within 3 seconds)\n    if len(events) > 1:\n        events_sorted = sorted(events, key=lambda e: float(e['time'].timestamp))\n        unique_events = [events_sorted[0]]\n\n        for evt in events_sorted[1:]:\n            last_evt = unique_events[-1]\n            time_diff = float(evt['time'].timestamp) - float(last_evt['time'].timestamp)\n\n            # If more than 3 seconds apart, consider it a new event\n            if time_diff > 3.0:\n                unique_events.append(evt)\n\n        events = unique_events\n        print(f\"After deduplication: {len(events)} unique events\")\n\n    return events\n\ndef write_results(events, output_file):\n    \"\"\"Write results to CSV.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nWriting {len(events)} events to {output_file}...\")\n\n    if not events:\n        pd.DataFrame(columns=['time']).to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n        print(f\"Results written to {output_file}\")\n        return\n\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n\n    # Load data\n    stream, stations_df = load_data()\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(stream)} traces from {len(set([t.stats.station for t in stream]))} stations\")\n\n    # Pick phases\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"PHASE PICKING\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    picks = pick_phases_optimized(stream)\n    if picks is None:\n        picks = []\n\n    print(f\"Picked {len(picks)} phases\")\n\n    if picks:\n        picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n        print(\"\\nPhase pick summary:\")\n        print(picks_df.groupby('phase').size())\n        print(f\"Stations with picks: {picks_df['station'].nunique()}\")\n\n        #
